    SELECT "p"."PRODUCT_ID", "p"."BADGE", "p"."BADGE_COLOR", "p"."CATEGORY_ID", "p"."DESCRIPTION", "p"."IMAGE_URL", "p"."IS_DELETED", "p"."MANUFACTURER", "p"."MINIMUM_AGE", "p"."NAME", "p"."PRICE", "p"."RATING", "p"."REVIEWS", "p"."SEARCH_NAME", "p"."STOCK_QUANTITY"
    FROM "C##ORACLEDB2026"."PRODUCT" "p"
    WHERE ((((((NOT ("p"."IS_DELETED" <> 0 )) AND ((("p"."SEARCH_NAME" IS NOT NULL AND ("p"."SEARCH_NAME" LIKE :p_Format)) OR (LOWER("p"."NAME") LIKE :p_Format3))))) AND ("p"."PRICE" >= :p_minPrice_Value))) AND ("p"."PRICE" <= :p_maxPrice_Value))
    ORDER BY "p"."NAME"
    OFFSET :p_p ROWS FETCH NEXT :p_p7 ROWS ONLY
) "p0"
INNER JOIN "C##ORACLEDB2026"."CATEGORY" "c" ON "p0"."CATEGORY_ID" = "c"."CATEGORY_ID"
ORDER BY "p0"."NAME"
